# Skills · P · part 14

834 entries, partcrafter to paseo-preview. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **partcrafter**: Generate structured, part-segmented 3D meshes from a single image using PartCrafter's compositional latent diffusion transformer. Use when you need 3D assets with explicit per-part labels (head, body, arms, etc.) ideal for rigging, animation, and game pipelines. Outputs a watertight mesh with per-vertex part annotations. MIT licensed. ([mpfaffenberger/skill-research](https://github.com/mpfaffenberger/skill-research/tree/HEAD/3d-mesh-design/round6/partcrafter) · ★15)
- **partcrafter-3d-mesh-generation**: Generates semantically-meaningful 3D parts from single images via compositional diffusion transformers with part-level identity and local-global attention. ([ADu2021/skillXiv](https://github.com/ADu2021/skillXiv/tree/HEAD/skills/skillxiv-v0.0.2-claude-opus-4.6/partcrafter-3d-mesh-generation) · ★7)
- **partdb**: Use when the user asks to find, organize, count, categorize, locate, or safely update components, tools, consumables, or other inventory in a Part-DB instance. Trigger for Part-DB inventory workflows, API or MCP integration choices, schema discovery, read-only stock lookup, intake planning, and proposed inventory changes. Do not use for a Part-DB container, proxy, backup, migration, or host operation; use `infra-ops` instead. ([cbusillo/codex-skills](https://github.com/cbusillo/codex-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/partdb) · ★5)
- **parte-especial-stj-sumulas**: Usar para pesquisar ou analisar: Parte Especial Penal, Súmulas STJ. Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/materia/penal/parte-especial-stj-sumulas))

Prüfraster: Darlehensgeberin Darlehensnehmerin vertretungsberechtigte Organe Handelsregisterstand. Output: Parteiendossier für Vertragskopf. Abgrenzung: Einstiegs-Skill; vor Vertragserstellung zu verwenden.
